The MARC 561$a shows 'אהרן בירב' but the authority evidence shows 'ביר, אהרן' with birth/death years. This name mismatch needs verification. Also, the P136 genre 'illuminated manuscript' is supported by MARC 655 'Illustrated works (Manuscript)' and 500$a mentions 'שער מעוטר' (decorated gate/title page).",

The P1932 value seems to mix these forms incorrectly. The description mentions 'Midrash' as subject which is supported by 650$a and 500$a, though P921 is not explicitly stated in the sample.
